# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram

```plantuml
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED

Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a booking for a customer on a given open flight with a list of passenger names, after validating there are no duplicate passengers and the flight hasn't departed yet.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null or f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  2. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  3. Check for duplicate passenger names in listOfPassengerNames (case-sensitive). If duplicates exist, return false.
  4. Create a new Booking object with this customer.
  5. For each passengerName in listOfPassengerNames:
       If there is already a reservation for this passenger on this flight (across all bookings), return false.
       Call booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now). If any call fails, return false.
  6. Add the new booking to this.bookings list.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::createReservation, Booking::reservations, Flight::reservations
Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
Input: f : Flight - the flight to book; now : EDate - current time; listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - list of passenger names.
Output: boolean - true if booking was created successfully, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Customer object exists.
Post-condition: If successful, a new Booking is added to the customer's bookings, and each passenger has a PENDING reservation on the flight. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation by its ID, provided the flight hasn't departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the reservation with the given reservationID across all bookings of this customer.
  2. If not found, return false.
  3. Let f = reservation.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  5. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If reservation.status is not PENDING, return false.
  7. Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::reservations, Reservation::status, Reservation::flight, Flight::openForBooking, Flight::departureTime
Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: reservationID : String - unique identifier of the reservation; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if reservation was confirmed, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: If successful, reservation.status is set to CONFIRMED. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation by its ID, provided the flight hasn't departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the reservation with the given reservationID across all bookings of this customer.
  2. If not found, return false.
  3. Let f = reservation.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  5. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If reservation.status is not CONFIRMED or PENDING, return false.
  7. Set reservation.status = CANCELED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::reservations, Reservation::status, Reservation::flight, Flight::openForBooking, Flight::departureTime
Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: reservationID : String - unique identifier of the reservation; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if reservation was canceled, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: If successful, reservation.status is set to CANCELED. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

```plantuml
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a booking for a customer on a given open flight with a list of passenger names, after validating there are no duplicate passengers and the flight hasn't departed yet.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null or f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  2. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  3. Check for duplicate passenger names in listOfPassengerNames (case-sensitive). If duplicates exist, return false.
  4. Create a new Booking object with this customer.
  5. For each passengerName in listOfPassengerNames:
       If there is already a reservation for this passenger on this flight (across all bookings), return false.
       Call booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now). If any call fails, return false.
  6. Add the new booking to this.bookings list.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::createReservation, Booking::reservations, Flight::reservations
Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
Input: f : Flight - the flight to book; now : EDate - current time; listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - list of passenger names.
Output: boolean - true if booking was created successfully, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Customer object exists.
Post-condition: If successful, a new Booking is added to the customer's bookings, and each passenger has a PENDING reservation on the flight. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation by its ID, provided the flight hasn't departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the reservation with the given reservationID across all bookings of this customer.
  2. If not found, return false.
  3. Let f = reservation.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  5. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If reservation.status is not PENDING, return false.
  7. Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::reservations, Reservation::status, Reservation::flight, Flight::openForBooking, Flight::departureTime
Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: reservationID : String - unique identifier of the reservation; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if reservation was confirmed, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: If successful, reservation.status is set to CONFIRMED. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation by its ID, provided the flight hasn't departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the reservation with the given reservationID across all bookings of this customer.
  2. If not found, return false.
  3. Let f = reservation.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking is false, return false.
  5. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If reservation.status is not CONFIRMED or PENDING, return false.
  7. Set reservation.status = CANCELED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>), Booking::reservations, Reservation::status, Reservation::flight, Flight::openForBooking, Flight::departureTime
Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: reservationID : String - unique identifier of the reservation; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if reservation was canceled, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: If successful, reservation.status is set to CANCELED. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a flight from the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Remove f from the flights list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight - the flight object to remove.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null and exists in flights list.
Post-condition: flights list does not contain f.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an open flight that has not yet departed, provided the current date is not the same calendar day as departure. Cancels all confirmed reservations on that flight.
Algorithm:
  1. Find flight with given flightId in flights list.
  2. If flight is null, return false.
  3. If flight.openForBooking is false, return false.
  4. If now is on the same calendar day as flight.departureTime, return false.
  5. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false.
  6. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  7. For each reservation in flight.reservations:
       If reservation.status == CONFIRMED, set reservation.status = CANCELED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>), Flight::openForBooking, Flight::reservations, Reservation::status
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: flightId : String - unique identifier of the flight; now : EDate - current date/time.
Output: boolean - true if flight was successfully closed, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: flightId corresponds to an existing flight in the airline.
Post-condition: If successful, flight.openForBooking is false, and all confirmed reservations on that flight are canceled. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings, after validating timestamps, temporal consistency, and route integrity.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking is true, return false.
  3. Validate f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are in "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss" format (assume EDate supports this).
  4. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  5. If f.departureTime >= f.arrivalTime, return false.
  6. If f.departureAirport == f.arrivalAirport, return false.
  7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>), Flight::openForBooking, Flight::departureTime, Flight::arrivalTime, Flight::departureAirport, Flight::arrivalAirport
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: f : Flight - the flight to publish; now : EDate - current date/time.
Output: boolean - true if flight was successfully published, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is a flight in the airline's flights list.
Post-condition: If successful, f.openForBooking is set to true. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a new flight to the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Add the flight f to the flights list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight - the flight object to add.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null and not already in flights list.
Post-condition: flights list contains f.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Searches for flights matching given origin airport code, destination airport code, and departure date.
Algorithm:
  1. Create an empty result list.
  2. For each flight in flights:
       If flight.departureAirport.id == origin AND flight.arrivalAirport.id == dest AND flight.departureTime's date component == date's date component:
           Add flight to result list.
  3. Return result list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>), Flight::departureAirport, Flight::arrivalAirport, Flight::departureTime
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: origin : String - departure airport ID; dest : String - arrival airport ID; date : EDate - departure date to match.
Output: List<Flight> - list of matching flights, possibly empty.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns list of flights that match all criteria.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

```plantuml
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}

    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}

}

    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}

    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean

    departureAirport : Airport
```

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary: Creates a single reservation for a passenger on a given flight. The reservation is initially set to PENDING status.
Algorithm:
  1. Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., using a counter or UUID).
  2. Create a new Reservation object with the generated ID, status = PENDING, flight = f, passenger = new Passenger(passenger).
  3. Add the reservation to this.reservations list.
  4. Add the reservation to f.reservations list.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: reservations (List<Reservation>), Flight::reservations
Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input: f : Flight - the flight for the reservation; passenger : String - passenger name; now : EDate - current time (used for validation elsewhere, but not directly here).
Output: boolean - true if reservation was created successfully.
Pre-condition: f is not null, passenger is not null/empty, and there is no existing reservation for this passenger on this flight in this booking.
Post-condition: A new reservation with PENDING status is added to both this booking's reservations and the flight's reservations. Returns true.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Flight::removeStopover
Summary: Removes a stopover from the flight, provided the flight hasn't departed yet.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null or not in stopovers list, return false.
  2. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false.
  3. Remove stop from stopovers list.
  4. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>), departureTime
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input: stop : Stopover - the stopover to remove; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if stopover was removed, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Flight exists.
Post-condition: If successful, stop is removed from the stopovers list. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary: Retrieves all confirmed reservations for this flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
Algorithm:
  1. If openForBooking is false, return an empty list.
  2. Filter reservations list to include only those with status == CONFIRMED.
  3. Return the filtered list.
Related Features: openForBooking (boolean), reservations (List<Reservation>)
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: None
Output: List<Reservation> - list of confirmed reservations, or empty list if flight is not open or no confirmed reservations exist
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns a list containing all reservations with status CONFIRMED if openForBooking is true; otherwise returns an empty list.

Flight::addStopover
Summary: Adds a stopover to the flight, provided the stopover's times are consistent with the flight's schedule.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false (cannot modify flight after departure).
  3. Validate stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime and stop.departureTime < flight.arrivalTime.
  4. If there are existing stopovers, ensure stop.arrivalTime > last stopover's departureTime.
  5. Add stop to stopovers list.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>), departureTime, arrivalTime
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input: stop : Stopover - the stopover to add; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if stopover was added, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Flight exists.
Post-condition: If successful, stop is added to the stopovers list. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: None
Output: List<Reservation> - list of confirmed reservations, or empty list if flight is not open or no confirmed reservations exist
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns a list containing all reservations with status CONFIRMED if openForBooking is true; otherwise returns an empty list.

Flight::addStopover
Summary: Adds a stopover to the flight, provided the stopover's times are consistent with the flight's schedule.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false (cannot modify flight after departure).
  3. Validate stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime and stop.departureTime < flight.arrivalTime.
  4. If there are existing stopovers, ensure stop.arrivalTime > last stopover's departureTime.
  5. Add stop to stopovers list.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>), departureTime, arrivalTime
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input: stop : Stopover - the stopover to add; now : EDate - current time.
Output: boolean - true if stopover was added, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Flight exists.
Post-condition: If successful, stop is added to the stopovers list. Returns true. Otherwise, returns false and no changes.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
  public boolean addStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. If stop is null or stop.airport is null, return false.
    if (stop == null || stop.getAirport() == null) {
      return false;
    }
    // 2. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false.
    if (!now.before(getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    // 3. Validate stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime and stop.departureTime < flight.arrivalTime.
    if (!(stop.getArrivalTime().before(stop.getDepartureTime()) && stop.getDepartureTime().before(getArrivalTime()))) {
      return false;
    }
    // 4. If there are existing stopovers, ensure stop.arrivalTime > last stopover's departureTime.

      if (!stop.getArrivalTime().after(lastStopover.getDepartureTime())) {

    getStopovers().add(stop);
    // 6. Return true.
    return true;
```

Step 3: "Validate stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime and stop.departureTime < flight.arrivalTime"

Actually, in the context of a stopover, it makes sense: the stopover has an arrival time (when the plane arrives at the stopover airport) and a departure time (when it leaves). The arrival at the stopover should be before the departure from the stopover. And the departure from the stopover should be before the flight's final arrival time.

Step 4: "ensure stop.arrivalTime > last stopover's departureTime" - the new stopover's arrival time should be after the last stopover's departure time (so they are in sequence).

```java
  public EList<Reservation> getConfirmedReservations()
  {
    // 1. If openForBooking is false, return an empty list.
    if (!isOpenForBooking()) {
      return new BasicEList<Reservation>();
    }
    // 2. Filter reservations list to include only those with status == CONFIRMED.
    EList<Reservation> confirmed = new BasicEList<Reservation>();

      if (reservation.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED) {
        confirmed.add(reservation);
      }
    }
    // 3. Return the filtered list.
    return confirmed;
  }
```

```java
public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
{
    // Step 1: Check that no reservation already exists for this passenger on this flight
    // Check in this booking's reservations
    for (Reservation r : getReservations()) {
        if (r.getFlight() == f && r.getPassenger() != null && passenger.equals(r.getPassenger().getName())) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    // Check in the flight's reservations
    for (Reservation r : f.getReservations()) {
        if (r.getPassenger() != null && passenger.equals(r.getPassenger().getName())) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Step 2: Generate a unique reservation ID
    String id = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    
    // Step 3: Create a new Reservation object
    Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    reservation.setId(id);
    reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    reservation.setFlight(f);
    Passenger p = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    p.setName(passenger);
    reservation.setPassenger(p);
    
    // Step 4: Add the reservation to this.reservations list
    getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // Step 5: Add the reservation to f.reservations list
    f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // Step 6: Return true
    return true;
}
```

1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary: Creates a booking for a customer on a specific open flight with a list of passenger names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check that f is not null and f.openForBooking is true.
   *   2. Check that now < f.departureTime.

   *   7. Return true.
```

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
{
    // Check that f is not null and f.openForBooking is true
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    // Check that now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    // For each passenger name, check for duplicates in f.reservations
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      for (Reservation reservation : f.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // Create a new Booking object
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    
    // For each passenger name, create a Reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      // Create a unique ID (using a simple approach)
      String uniqueId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
      
      // Create Passenger
      Passenger passenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
      passenger.setName(passengerName);
      
      // Create Reservation
      Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
      reservation.setId(uniqueId);
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
      reservation.setFlight(f);
      reservation.setPassenger(passenger);
      
      // Add reservation to booking's reservations and to f.reservations
      booking.getReservations().add(reservation);
      f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    }
    
    // Add the booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    
    return true;
}
```

For `confirm` and `cancel`, I need to search through all bookings of this customer to find the reservation with the given ID.

```java
public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
{
    // Find the reservation with the given reservationID in this customer's bookings
    Reservation foundReservation = null;
    for (Booking booking : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          foundReservation = reservation;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (foundReservation != null) break;
    }
    
    if (foundReservation == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Flight f = foundReservation.getFlight();
    
    // Check that f.openForBooking is true and now < f.departureTime
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking() || !now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
    foundReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
    
    return true;
}
```

```java
public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
{
    // Check that f is not null and f.openForBooking is true
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    // Check that now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    // For each passenger name, check for duplicates in f.reservations
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      for (Reservation reservation : f.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // Create a new Booking object
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    
    // For each passenger name, create a Reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      // Create a unique ID (using a simple approach)
      String uniqueId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
      
      // Create Passenger
      Passenger passenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
      passenger.setName(passengerName);
      
      // Create Reservation
      Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
      reservation.setId(uniqueId);
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
      reservation.setFlight(f);
      reservation.setPassenger(passenger);
      
      // Add reservation to booking's reservations and to f.reservations
      booking.getReservations().add(reservation);
      f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    }
    
    // Add the booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    
    return true;
}
```

```java
public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
{
    // Find the reservation with the given reservationID in this customer's bookings
    Reservation foundReservation = null;
    for (Booking booking : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          foundReservation = reservation;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (foundReservation != null) break;
    }
    
    if (foundReservation == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Flight f = foundReservation.getFlight();
    
    // Check that f.openForBooking is true and now < f.departureTime
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking() || !now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
    foundReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
    
    return true;
}
```

   * Post-condition: If return is true, a new Booking is added to this customer's bookings list, and for each passenger name a Reservation with status PENDING is added to the booking's reservations list and to f.reservations. If return is false, no changes are made.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Check that f is not null and f.openForBooking is true
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    // Check that now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    // For each passenger name, check for duplicates in f.reservations
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      for (Reservation reservation : f.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // Create a new Booking object for this customer
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    
    // For each passenger name, create a Reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      // Create a unique ID
      String uniqueId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
      
      // Create a Passenger with the given name
      Passenger passenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
      passenger.setName(passengerName);
      
      // Create a new Reservation with unique ID, status = PENDING, flight = f, and passenger
      Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
      reservation.setId(uniqueId);
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
      reservation.setFlight(f);
      reservation.setPassenger(passenger);
      
      // Add the reservation to the booking's reservations list and to f.reservations
      booking.getReservations().add(reservation);
      f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    }
    
    // Add the booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Confirms an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Find the reservation with the given reservationID in this customer's bookings.
   *   2. If not found, return false.
   *   3. Let f = reservation.flight.
   *   4. Check that f.openForBooking is true and now < f.departureTime.
   *   5. If check fails, return false.
   *   6. Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input: reservationID : String - the unique ID of the reservation to confirm; now : EDate - current date/time
   * Output: boolean - true if confirmation successful, false otherwise
   * Pre-condition: reservationID is not null; now is a valid EDate.
   * Post-condition: If return is true, the reservation's status is set to CONFIRMED. If return is false, no changes are made.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Find the reservation with the given reservationID in this customer's bookings
    Reservation foundReservation = null;
    for (Booking booking : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          foundReservation = reservation;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (foundReservation != null) {
        break;
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    if (foundReservation == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Let f = reservation.flight
    Flight f = foundReservation.getFlight();
    
    // Check that f.openForBooking is true and now < f.departureTime
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking() || !now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
    foundReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
    
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Cancels an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Find the reservation with the given reservationID in this customer's bookings
    Reservation foundReservation = null;
    for (Booking booking : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getId().equals(reservationID)) {

          break;

      if (foundReservation != null) {
        break;
      }
    }

    if (foundReservation == null) {
      return false;

    }
    
    // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
    foundReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
    
    return true;

The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
```java
   *   1. Find the flight with flightId in this airline's flights list.
   *   2. If not found, return false.
   *   3. Let f = the found flight.

   *   5. Check that now's calendar date is not the same as f.departureTime's calendar date (i.e., they are not on the same day).
   *   6. If any check fails, return false.
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = false.
   *   8. For each reservation r in f.reservations:

   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input: flightId : String - the unique ID of the flight to close; now : EDate - current date/time
   * Output: boolean - true if closing successful, false otherwise
   * Pre-condition: flightId is not null; now is a valid EDate.
   * Post-condition: If return is true, f.openForBooking is set to false and all confirmed reservations in f are canceled. If return is false, no changes are made.
```

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
```java
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check that f is not null.
   *   2. Check that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   *   3. Check that now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime.
   *   4. Check that f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport.
   *   5. Check that f.openForBooking is false.
   *   6. If any check fails, return false.
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
   *   8. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight
   * Input: f : Flight - the flight to publish; now : EDate - current date/time
```

```java
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
   *   8. Return true.
```

```java
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean publishFlight(final Flight f, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Check that f is not null.
    if (f == null) return false;

    SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);

    // 2. Check that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
    Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    Date arrivalTime = f.getArrivalTime();
    if (departureTime == null || arrivalTime == null) return false;

    try {
      sdf.format(departureTime); // This won't throw but we need to parse the format
      // Actually, to validate format, we should try parsing the string representation
      String depStr = sdf.format(departureTime);
      String arrStr = sdf.format(arrivalTime);
      // Re-parse to validate
      sdf.parse(depStr);
      sdf.parse(arrStr);
    } catch (Exception e) {
      return false;
    }

    // 3. Check that now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime.
    if (!(now.before(departureTime) && departureTime.before(arrivalTime))) {
      return false;
    }

    // 4. Check that f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport.
    Airport depAirport = f.getDepartureAirport();
    Airport arrAirport = f.getArrivalAirport();

    if (depAirport.getId().equals(arrAirport.getId())) return false;

    // 5. Check that f.openForBooking is false.
    if (f.isOpenForBooking()) return false;

    // 7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
    f.setOpenForBooking(true);

    // 8. Return true.
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Closes an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Find the flight with flightId in this airline's flights list.
   *   2. If not found, return false.
   *   3. Let f = the found flight.
   *   4. Check that f.openForBooking is true.
   *   5. Check that now's calendar date is not the same as f.departureTime's calendar date (i.e., they are not on the same day).
   *   6. If any check fails, return false.
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = false.
   *   8. For each reservation r in f.reservations:
   *      a. If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input: flightId : String - the unique ID of the flight to close; now : EDate - current date/time

   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Find the flight with flightId in this airline's flights list.
    Flight f = null;
    for (Flight flight : getFlights()) {
      if (flight.getId() != null && flight.getId().equals(flightId)) {
        f = flight;
        break;
      }
    }

    // 2. If not found, return false.
    if (f == null) return false;

    // 4. Check that f.openForBooking is true.
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) return false;

    // 5. Check that now's calendar date is not the same as f.departureTime's calendar date.
    Calendar nowCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    nowCal.setTime(now);
    Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    depCal.setTime(f.getDepartureTime());

    if (nowCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
        nowCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR)) {
      return false;
    }

    // 7. Set f.openForBooking = false.
    f.setOpenForBooking(false);

    // 8. For each reservation r in f.reservations:
    for (Reservation r : f.getReservations()) {
      // a. If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED) {
        r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }

    // 9. Return true.
    return true;
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Searches for flights matching given origin airport code, destination airport code, and departure date.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Create an empty result list.

   *        If flight.departureAirport.id == origin AND flight.arrivalAirport.id == dest AND flight.departureTime's date component == date's date component:
   *            Add flight to result list.
   *   3. Return result list.

   * Output: List<Flight> - list of matching flights, possibly empty.
   * Pre-condition: None.
   * Post-condition: Returns list of flights that match all criteria.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    // 1. Create an empty result list.
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<Flight>();

    // 2. For each flight in flights:
    for (Flight flight : getFlights()) {
      Airport depAirport = flight.getDepartureAirport();
      Airport arrAirport = flight.getArrivalAirport();

      // If flight.departureAirport.id == origin AND flight.arrivalAirport.id == dest
      // AND flight.departureTime's date component == date's date component
      if (depAirport != null && arrAirport != null &&
          origin.equals(depAirport.getId()) &&
          dest.equals(arrAirport.getId()) &&
          isSameDay(flight.getDepartureTime(), date)) {
        // Add flight to result list.
        result.add(flight);
      }
    }

    // 3. Return result list.
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Helper method to check if two dates fall on the same calendar day.
   */
  private boolean isSameDay(Date date1, Date date2) {
    if (date1 == null || date2 == null) return false;
    Calendar cal1 = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal1.setTime(date1);
    Calendar cal2 = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal2.setTime(date2);
    return cal1.get(Calendar.YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
           cal1.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR);
  }
```

   * Post-condition: Returns list of flights that match all criteria.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    // 1. Create an empty result list.
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<Flight>();

    // 2. For each flight in flights:
    for (Flight flight : getFlights())
    {
      Airport depAirport = flight.getDepartureAirport();
      Airport arrAirport = flight.getArrivalAirport();

      if (depAirport != null && arrAirport != null &&
          origin.equals(depAirport.getId()) &&
          dest.equals(arrAirport.getId()) &&
          isSameDay(flight.getDepartureTime(), date))
      {
        // Add flight to result list.
        result.add(flight);
      }
    }

    // 3. Return result list.
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Helper method to check if two dates fall on the same calendar day.
   */
  private boolean isSameDay(Date date1, Date date2)
  {
    if (date1 == null || date2 == null) return false;
    Calendar cal1 = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal1.setTime(date1);
    Calendar cal2 = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal2.setTime(date2);
    return cal1.get(Calendar.YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
           cal1.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Publishes a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check that f is not null.
   *   2. Check that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   *   3. Check that now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime.
   *   4. Check that f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport.
   *   5. Check that f.openForBooking is false.
   *   6. If any check fails, return false.
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
   *   8. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight
   * Input: f : Flight - the flight to publish; now : EDate - current date/time
   * Output: boolean - true if publication successful, false otherwise
   * Pre-condition: f is not null; now is a valid EDate.
   * Post-condition: If return is true, f.openForBooking is set to true. If return is false, no changes are made.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean publishFlight(final Flight f, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Check that f is not null.
    if (f == null) return false;

    // 2. Check that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
    Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    Date arrivalTime = f.getArrivalTime();
    if (departureTime == null || arrivalTime == null) return false;

    SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);
    try
    {
      String depStr = sdf.format(departureTime);
      sdf.parse(depStr);
      String arrStr = sdf.format(arrivalTime);
      sdf.parse(arrStr);
    }
    catch (Exception e)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 3. Check that now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime.
    if (!(now.before(departureTime) && departureTime.before(arrivalTime)))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 4. Check that f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport.
    Airport depAirport = f.getDepartureAirport();
    Airport arrAirport = f.getArrivalAirport();
    if (depAirport == null || arrAirport == null) return false;
    String depId = depAirport.getId();
    String arrId = arrAirport.getId();
    if (depId == null || arrId == null || depId.equals(arrId)) return false;

    // 5. Check that f.openForBooking is false.
    if (f.isOpenForBooking()) return false;

    // 7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
    f.setOpenForBooking(true);

    // 8. Return true.
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Closes an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Find the flight with flightId in this airline's flights list.
   *   2. If not found, return false.
   *   3. Let f = the found flight.
   *   4. Check that f.openForBooking is true.
   *   5. Check that now's calendar date is not the same as f.departureTime's calendar date (i.e., they are not on the same day).
   *   6. If any check fails, return false.
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = false.
   *   8. For each reservation r in f.reservations:
   *      a. If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input: flightId : String - the unique ID of the flight to close; now : EDate - current date/time
   * Output: boolean - true if closing successful, false otherwise
   * Pre-condition: flightId is not null; now is a valid EDate.
   * Post-condition: If return is true, f.openForBooking is set to false and all confirmed reservations in f are canceled. If return is false, no changes are made.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Find the flight with flightId in this airline's flights list.
    Flight f = null;
    for (Flight flight : getFlights())
    {
      if (flight.getId() != null && flight.getId().equals(flightId))
      {
        f = flight;
        break;
      }
    }

    // 2. If not found, return false.
    if (f == null) return false;

    // 4. Check that f.openForBooking is true.
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) return false;

    // 5. Check that now's calendar date is not the same as f.departureTime's calendar date.
    Calendar nowCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    nowCal.setTime(now);
    Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    depCal.setTime(f.getDepartureTime());

    if (nowCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
        nowCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 7. Set f.openForBooking = false.
    f.setOpenForBooking(false);

    // 8. For each reservation r in f.reservations:
    for (Reservation r : f.getReservations())
    {
      // a. If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED)
      {
        r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }

    // 9. Return true.
    return true;
  }
